```python
def _kinds_before_their_phase(model_dir):
```

por:

```python
# wasichai-documents 0.6.0 (DocumentTypeService): a type's name, its printed sigla and what a template may name
DOCUMENT_TYPE_NAME = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,48}$")
DOCUMENT_PREFIX = re.compile(r"^[A-Z][A-Z0-9]{0,11}$")
PLATFORM_VALUES = ("today", "now", "user", "id", "documentName", "documentPrefix", "documentSerial", "documentNumber")
MAX_TEMPLATE_NODES = 5000

# wasichai-automation 0.6.0 (AutomationService): a name is unique in the organization, whatever its object
AUTOMATION_NAME = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,48}$")
TRIGGER_TYPES = ("RECORD_CREATED", "RECORD_UPDATED", "RECORD_DELETED", "TRANSITION_APPLIED", "STATE_ENTERED")
OPERATORS = ("EQUALS", "NOT_EQUALS", "GREATER_THAN", "LESS_THAN", "CONTAINS", "IS_EMPTY", "IS_NOT_EMPTY", "CHANGED")
VALUELESS = ("IS_EMPTY", "IS_NOT_EMPTY", "CHANGED")
ACTION_TYPES = ("UPDATE_FIELD", "CREATE_RECORD", "GENERATE_DOCUMENT")
# workflows.md, «Automatizaciones»: no WEBHOOK in the MVP (GAP-020), and sgspe.notification decides who is told, by
# project assignment (ASM-070): a NOTIFY to a role would reach every holder of the role in the tenant
REFUSED_ACTIONS = {
    "WEBHOOK": "no WEBHOOK in the MVP (GAP-020)",
    "NOTIFY": "sgspe.notification tells people by project assignment (ASM-070), never NOTIFY",
}


def _record_fields(model, object_name):
    """{name: field} of what a record of the object holds: its own fields and the field of each MANY_TO_ONE relationship
    it is the source of (type RELATION)."""
    obj = next(o for o in model["objects"] if o["name"] == object_name)
    fields = {f["name"]: f for f in obj["fields"]}
    for rel in model["relationships"]:
        if rel["source"] == object_name and rel.get("fieldName"):
            fields[rel["fieldName"]] = {"name": rel["fieldName"], "type": "RELATION", "required": rel.get("required", False)}
    return fields


def _list_of_objects(label, items):
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        return [f"{label}: the file must be a JSON list"]
    if not all(isinstance(item, dict) for item in items):
        return [f"{label}: every entry must be a JSON object"]
    return []


def _template_errors(label, root, fields, relationships):
    """What wasichai-documents would refuse in a template: a root that is not a doc, a node that is not an object, an
    objectField, relatedTable or platformValue naming something that does not exist, and more than 5000 nodes."""
    if not isinstance(root, dict) or root.get("type") != "doc":
        return [f"{label}: the template root must be a node of type doc"]
    errors, seen = [], 0
    stack = [root]
    while stack:
        node = stack.pop()
        seen += 1
        if not isinstance(node, dict) or not isinstance(node.get("type"), str):
            errors.append(f"{label}: every template node must be an object with a type")
            continue
        attrs = node.get("attrs") or {}
        if node["type"] == "objectField" and attrs.get("field") not in fields:
            errors.append(f"{label}: objectField names {attrs.get('field')!r}, which is not a field of the object")
        if node["type"] == "relatedTable" and attrs.get("relationship") not in relationships:
            errors.append(f"{label}: relatedTable names {attrs.get('relationship')!r}, which is not a relationship of the object")
        if node["type"] == "platformValue" and attrs.get("value") not in PLATFORM_VALUES:
            errors.append(f"{label}: platformValue {attrs.get('value')!r} must be one of {', '.join(PLATFORM_VALUES)}")
        content = node.get("content") or []
        if not isinstance(content, list):
            errors.append(f"{label}: the content of a {node['type']} node must be a list")
            continue
        stack.extend(content)
    if seen > MAX_TEMPLATE_NODES:
        errors.append(f"{label}: the template holds {seen} nodes, more than {MAX_TEMPLATE_NODES}")
    return errors


def validate_document_types(model, object_name, types):
    """The errors of model/document-types/<object>.json against model.json and wasichai-documents's rules."""
    label = f"document types of {object_name}"
    if object_name not in {o["name"] for o in model["objects"]}:
        return [f"{label}: object '{object_name}' is not in model.json"]
    errors = _list_of_objects(label, types)
    if errors:
        return errors
    fields = _record_fields(model, object_name)
    relationships = {r["name"] for r in model["relationships"] if object_name in (r["source"], r["target"])}
    names = set()
    for doc in types:
        name = doc.get("name")
        where = f"document type {object_name}.{name}"
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not DOCUMENT_TYPE_NAME.fullmatch(name):
            errors.append(f"{where}: name must match {DOCUMENT_TYPE_NAME.pattern}")
        if name in names:
            errors.append(f"{where}: the name repeats")
        names.add(name)
        if not isinstance(doc.get("label"), str) or not doc["label"] or _edge_spaces(doc["label"]):
            errors.append(f"{where}: label is missing or has leading or trailing spaces")
        if not isinstance(doc.get("prefix"), str) or not DOCUMENT_PREFIX.fullmatch(doc["prefix"]):
            errors.append(f"{where}: prefix must match {DOCUMENT_PREFIX.pattern}")
        errors.extend(_template_errors(where, doc.get("template"), fields, relationships))
    return errors


def _state_names(workflow):
    return {s["name"] for s in (workflow or {}).get("definition", {}).get("states", [])}


def _transition_names(workflow):
    return {t["name"] for t in (workflow or {}).get("definition", {}).get("transitions", [])}


def _action_errors(where, action, model, object_name, fields, document_types):
    kind = action.get("type")
    if kind in REFUSED_ACTIONS:
        return [f"{where}: {kind} is not used: {REFUSED_ACTIONS[kind]}"]
    if kind not in ACTION_TYPES:
        return [f"{where}: action type must be one of {', '.join(ACTION_TYPES)}"]
    if kind == "GENERATE_DOCUMENT":
        if action.get("documentType") not in document_types:
            return [f"{where}: documentType {action.get('documentType')!r} is not a document type of {object_name}"]
        return []
    if kind == "UPDATE_FIELD":
        field = fields.get(action.get("field"))
        if field is None:
            return [f"{where}: UPDATE_FIELD names {action.get('field')!r}, which is not a field of {object_name}"]
        if field.get("derived"):
            return [f"{where}: UPDATE_FIELD writes the derived field {field['name']} (DerivedFieldsGuard refuses it)"]
        if not isinstance(action.get("value"), str):
            return [f"{where}: UPDATE_FIELD needs a text value"]
        return []
    target = action.get("targetObject")
    if target not in {o["name"] for o in model["objects"]}:
        return [f"{where}: CREATE_RECORD targets {target!r}, which is not in model.json"]
    values = action.get("values")
    if not isinstance(values, dict) or not all(isinstance(v, str) for v in values.values()):
        return [f"{where}: CREATE_RECORD values must be an object of texts"]
    target_fields = _record_fields(model, target)
    errors = []
    for name in values:
        if name not in target_fields:
            errors.append(f"{where}: CREATE_RECORD sets {target}.{name}, which does not exist")
        elif target_fields[name].get("derived"):
            errors.append(f"{where}: CREATE_RECORD sets the derived field {target}.{name} (DerivedFieldsGuard refuses it)")
    for name, field in target_fields.items():
        if field.get("required") and "defaultValue" not in field and name not in values:
            errors.append(f"{where}: CREATE_RECORD leaves out {target}.{name}, which is required")
    return errors


def validate_automations(model, object_name, automations, workflow, document_types):
    """The errors of model/automations/<object>.json against model.json, the object's workflow and document types, and
    wasichai-automation's rules."""
    label = f"automations of {object_name}"
    if object_name not in {o["name"] for o in model["objects"]}:
        return [f"{label}: object '{object_name}' is not in model.json"]
    errors = _list_of_objects(label, automations)
    if errors:
        return errors
    fields = _record_fields(model, object_name)
    for automation in automations:
        name = automation.get("name")
        where = f"automation {name}"
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not AUTOMATION_NAME.fullmatch(name):
            errors.append(f"{where}: name must match {AUTOMATION_NAME.pattern}")
        if not isinstance(automation.get("label"), str) or not automation["label"] or _edge_spaces(automation["label"]):
            errors.append(f"{where}: label is missing or has leading or trailing spaces")
        if automation.get("enabled") is not True:
            errors.append(f"{where}: enabled must be true (an automation that waits is left out of model/)")
        definition = automation.get("definition")
        if not isinstance(definition, dict) or not isinstance(definition.get("trigger"), dict):
            errors.append(f"{where}: definition must be an object with a trigger")
            continue
        trigger = definition["trigger"]
        if trigger.get("type") not in TRIGGER_TYPES:
            errors.append(f"{where}: trigger type must be one of {', '.join(TRIGGER_TYPES)}")
        elif trigger["type"] == "STATE_ENTERED" and trigger.get("state") not in _state_names(workflow):
            errors.append(f"{where}: STATE_ENTERED names {trigger.get('state')!r}, which is not a state of {object_name}")
        elif trigger["type"] == "TRANSITION_APPLIED" and trigger.get("transition") is not None \
                and trigger["transition"] not in _transition_names(workflow):
            errors.append(f"{where}: TRANSITION_APPLIED names {trigger['transition']!r}, which is not a transition of "
                          f"{object_name}")
        conditions = definition.get("conditions", [])
        actions = definition.get("actions")
        if not isinstance(conditions, list) or not all(isinstance(c, dict) for c in conditions):
            errors.append(f"{where}: conditions must be a list of objects")
            conditions = []
        for condition in conditions:
            if condition.get("field") != "state" and condition.get("field") not in fields:
                errors.append(f"{where}: a condition names {condition.get('field')!r}, which is not a field of {object_name}")
            if condition.get("operator") not in OPERATORS:
                errors.append(f"{where}: a condition operator must be one of {', '.join(OPERATORS)}")
            elif condition["operator"] not in VALUELESS and not condition.get("value"):
                errors.append(f"{where}: a {condition['operator']} condition needs a value")
        if not isinstance(actions, list) or not actions or not all(isinstance(a, dict) for a in actions):
            errors.append(f"{where}: actions must be a non-empty list of objects")
            continue
        for action in actions:
            errors.extend(_action_errors(where, action, model, object_name, fields, document_types))
    return errors


def _load_json(model_dir, path, errors):
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(model_dir, path), encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except ValueError as e:
        errors.append(f"model/{path}: not valid JSON: {e}")
        return None


def load_definitions(model_dir, model):
    """Everything --workflows applies, checked: (workflows, document types, automations, errors), where the document
    types and the automations are (object, definition) pairs in file order. Names and prefixes that wasichai keeps
    unique in the organization are checked across files."""
    workflows, errors = load_workflows(model_dir, model)
    by_object = dict(workflows)
    types, automations = [], []
    for path in definition_files(model_dir):
        kind, name = path.split("/")
        if kind not in ("document-types", "automations"):
            continue
        object_name = name[:-len(".json")]
        loaded = _load_json(model_dir, path, errors)
        if loaded is None:
            continue
        if kind == "document-types":
            found = validate_document_types(model, object_name, loaded)
            errors.extend(f"model/{path}: {e}" for e in found)
            if not found:
                types.extend((object_name, doc) for doc in loaded)
    names_by_object = {}
    for object_name, doc in types:
        names_by_object.setdefault(object_name, set()).add(doc["name"])
    for path in definition_files(model_dir):
        kind, name = path.split("/")
        if kind != "automations":
            continue
        object_name = name[:-len(".json")]
        loaded = _load_json(model_dir, path, errors)
        if loaded is None:
            continue
        found = validate_automations(model, object_name, loaded, by_object.get(object_name),
                                     names_by_object.get(object_name, set()))
        errors.extend(f"model/{path}: {e}" for e in found)
        if not found:
            automations.extend((object_name, automation) for automation in loaded)
    prefixes = [doc["prefix"] for _, doc in types]
    errors.extend(f"model/document-types: prefix {p} is used by two types (wasichai keeps one per organization)"
                  for p in sorted({p for p in prefixes if prefixes.count(p) > 1}))
    names = [a["name"] for _, a in automations]
    errors.extend(f"model/automations: automation {n} is declared twice (wasichai keeps one per organization)"
                  for n in sorted({n for n in names if names.count(n) > 1}))
    return workflows, types, automations, errors


def _compact(value):
    """A definition as wasichai answers it, comparable with the file: no null keys and no empty objects (the keys an
    answer adds that the file leaves out)."""
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: _compact(v) for k, v in value.items() if v is not None and v != {}}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_compact(v) for v in value]
    return value


def _document_type_body(doc):
    return {"label": doc["label"], "prefix": doc["prefix"], "template": doc["template"]}


def _automation_body(automation):
    return {"label": automation["label"], "enabled": automation["enabled"], "definition": automation["definition"]}


def _sync_named(client, kind, object_name, name, wanted, collection):
    """Create (POST) the named definition Core lacks, replace (PUT) the one that differs, skip the equal one. Returns
    'created', 'updated', 'skipped', or None when Core refused (already reported)."""
    path = f"{collection}/{name}"
    method, target = "GET", path
    try:
        current = client.get(path)
        if current is not None and _compact({k: current.get(k) for k in wanted}) == _compact(wanted):
            print(f"skip   {kind} {object_name}.{name} (up to date)")
            return "skipped"
        if current is None:
            method, target = "POST", collection
            client.post(collection, {"name": name, **wanted})
            print(f"create {kind} {object_name}.{name}")
            return "created"
        method = "PUT"
        client.put(path, {"name": name, **wanted})
        print(f"update {kind} {object_name}.{name}")
        return "updated"
    except CoreError as e:
        _fatal(method, target, e)
        return None


def _warn_extra_automations(client, automations):
    """An automation Core runs and model/ no longer declares keeps running: reported on stderr, never deleted."""
    declared = {a["name"] for _, a in automations}
    for object_name in sorted({o for o, _ in automations}):
        for current in client.get(f"/api/objects/{object_name}/automations") or []:
            if current.get("name") not in declared:
                print(f"warning: automation {current.get('name')} of {object_name} is in core but not in model/; left as is "
                      f"(delete it in /admin if it is a leftover)", file=sys.stderr)


def _kinds_before_their_phase(model_dir):
```

```python
    """The workflows of model/workflows/: a missing one is created, one that differs is replaced (PUT is the whole
    api), an equal one is skipped. A definition of a kind that has not arrived yet is an error, never a silent skip.
    Nothing is written until every workflow is checked against the records: one that would strand records (it drops a
    state they sit in) stops the pass with 1, and so does one created over records unless allow_stateless_records."""
    if _kinds_before_their_phase(model_dir):
        return 2
    workflows, errors = load_workflows(model_dir, model)
```

### Tarea 4: El modelo de F3: `model.json`, workflows y `roles.json`

Los tres van juntos: las pruebas del modelo y de los roles ya existen y derivan la fase del objeto más tardío del modelo, así
que un `model.json` de F3 sin los workflows ni las concesiones de F3 las dejaría en rojo.

**Files:**
- Create (fuera del repositorio, no se versionan): `$SCRATCH/gen_model_f3.py`, `$SCRATCH/gen_roles_f3.py`
- Modify: `model/model.json` y `model/roles.json` (generados una vez)
- Create: `model/workflows/commitment.json`, `agreement.json`, `commitment_candidate.json`, `task.json`
- Modify: `model/test_model.py`, `model/test_apply.py`, `model/test_apply_roles.py`
- Modify: `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/common/GuardTablesCatalogTest.kt`, `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/F2ApiTest.kt`
- Modify (documentos): `docs/security.md` §7.5 (`PROJECT_ADMIN` en `task`), `docs/workflows.md` (WF-N, fase de cada rol)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `design_docs.cards()` (con `content_types`), `catalogs()`, `matrix()`, `role_phases()`,
  `restricted_fields()`, `workflow_transitions(letter, obj)` (Tarea 2); `apply.validate`, `load_definitions` (Tarea 3).
- Produces: los nombres de «Interfaces», secciones «El modelo», «Los workflows» y «Roles y permisos»;
  `F2ApiTest.stagedFile(objectName, field, account)` y `F2ApiTest.PDF`; las entradas de F3 de `EXACTLY_ONE` y `FIXED`.

`$SCRATCH` es el scratchpad de la sesión de quien ejecuta, fuera de `~/IdeaProjects/sgspe`: por ejemplo,
`export SCRATCH=<scratchpad de la sesión>/f3-1 && mkdir -p "$SCRATCH"`.

```python
    def test_a_tenant_of_f2_moves_to_f3_adding_only_what_f3_brings(self):
        # Review Focus 1: el modelo de F2 sale de model.json con las fases de las fichas (168 creados y 11 conjuntos, como
        # el aprovisionamiento real de F2); subirlo a F3 agrega 12 objetos, 15 campos de project, 6 acciones y 46
        # relaciones, pone los únicos de 4 objetos nuevos y no toca nada de F2
        core = FakeCore()
        self.addCleanup(core.stop)
        login = ["--core", core.base_url, "--email", "admin-tenant@sgspe.test", "--password", "clave-del-tenant"]
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            f2 = quiet(apply.main, ["--model", write_model(folder, f2_subset(load(MODEL_PATH))), *login])
        self.assertEqual(f2[0], 0, f2)
        self.assertIn("done: 168 created, 11 updated, 0 skipped", f2[1])
        core.requests.clear()
        f3 = quiet(apply.main, ["--model", MODEL_PATH, *login])
        self.assertEqual(f3[0], 0, f3)
        self.assertIn("done: 79 created, 4 updated, 167 skipped", f3[1])
        touched = {p.split("/")[3] for m, p, _, _ in core.requests if m == "PUT" and p.startswith("/api/objects/")}
        self.assertEqual(touched, {"commitment", "commitment_dependency", "agreement", "agreement_clause"})
        added = [line for line in f3[1].splitlines() if line.startswith("add    field")]
        self.assertEqual(len(added), 15)
        self.assertTrue(all(line.startswith("add    field project.") for line in added), added)


def f2_subset(model):
    """model.json as it was in F2: the objects, fields, relationships and ENUM keys whose phase is F2 by the cards."""
    import design_docs
    cards = design_docs.cards()
    objects = []
    for obj in model["objects"]:
        card = cards[obj["name"]]
        if card.phase > "F2":
            continue
        phases = {f["name"]: design_docs.field_phase(card, f) for f in card.fields}
        objects.append({**obj, "fields": [f for f in obj["fields"] if phases[f["name"]] <= "F2"]})
    names = {o["name"] for o in objects}
    relationships = [r for r in model["relationships"] if r["source"] in names and r["target"] in names
                     and design_docs.relationship_phase(cards, cards[r["source"]],
                                                        next(x for x in cards[r["source"]].relationships
                                                             if x["name"] == r["name"])) <= "F2"]
    used = {f["enum"] for o in objects for f in o["fields"] if f["type"] == "ENUM"}
    return {"enums": {k: v for k, v in model["enums"].items() if k in used}, "objects": objects,
            "relationships": relationships}
```

```python
        # security.md §5.3, «Doble llave en las decisiones»: en WF-D, approve (APPROVER) y approve_senior
        # (PROJECT_MANAGER) son las dos aprobaciones, y verify la verificación (D3, D11)
        for obj, transitions, action in (("project", ("approve",), "APPROVE"), ("area_of_influence", ("approve",), "APPROVE"),
                                         ("area_of_influence", ("supersede",), "APPROVE"),
                                         ("commitment", ("approve", "approve_senior"), "APPROVE"),
                                         ("commitment", ("verify",), "VERIFY")):
            workflow = load(os.path.join(HERE, "workflows", f"{obj}.json"))
            roles = {r for t in workflow["definition"]["transitions"] if t["name"] in transitions for r in t["roles"]}
            holders = {name for name, role in self.roles.items() if action in role["permisos"].get(obj, [])}
            self.assertEqual(roles, holders, (obj, transitions))

    def test_quien_supera_el_tope_tiene_la_aprobacion_superior(self):
        # workflows.md, WF-D (D7): APPROVE_OVER_CAP solo sirve con una transición de aprobación; en commitment la tiene
        # PROJECT_MANAGER, el rol de approve_senior, y ningún rol de approve ni de approve_direct
        workflow = load(os.path.join(HERE, "workflows", "commitment.json"))
        roles = {t["name"]: set(t["roles"]) for t in workflow["definition"]["transitions"]}
        holders = {name for name, role in self.roles.items() if "APPROVE_OVER_CAP" in role["permisos"].get("commitment", [])}
        self.assertEqual(holders, roles["approve_senior"])
        self.assertEqual(holders & (roles["approve"] | roles["approve_direct"]), set())

    def test_quien_crea_un_objeto_con_exactamente_uno_de_lee_algun_destino_de_cada_grupo(self):
        # ExactlyOneGuard pide un destino de cada grupo y wasichai responde 400 a un valor RELATION que quien escribe
        # no lee (security.md §5.6): quien crea debe leer al menos uno de los que existen en la fase
        model = load(MODEL_PATH)
        cards = design_docs.cards()
        targets = {(r["source"], r["fieldName"]): r["target"] for r in model["relationships"] if r.get("fieldName")}
        for name, role in self.roles.items():
            for obj in (o["name"] for o in model["objects"]):
                if "CREATE" not in role["permisos"].get(obj, []):
                    continue
                for group in cards[obj].exactly_one:
                    present = [targets[(obj, f)] for f in group if (obj, f) in targets]
                    readable = [t for t in present if "READ" in role["permisos"].get(t, [])]
                    self.assertTrue(readable, (name, obj, group))
```

- [ ] **Paso 4: Guardar el generador del modelo**

Crear `$SCRATCH/gen_model_f3.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""One-shot generator of model/model.json for phase F3, from the cards of docs/database-model.md (plan 3.1, Tarea 4).

Run from the repository root: python3 <this file> > model/model.f3.json && mv model/model.f3.json model/model.json
Stdlib only. Keeps every object, field and relationship model.json already has exactly as it is (F2 was edited by hand
after its generator), adds the F3 ones in the order of the cards with the label rule of database-model.md §3.8 and the
shortened labels below, and prints the model in the hand layout of model.json (one field or relationship per line).
"""
import json
import os
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "model"))
import design_docs as d  # noqa: E402

PHASE = "F3"

PLURAL = {
    "commitment": "Compromisos", "commitment_action": "Acciones del compromiso",
    "commitment_milestone": "Hitos del compromiso", "commitment_reschedule": "Reprogramaciones",
    "commitment_dependency": "Dependencias entre compromisos", "delivery": "Entregas",
    "commitment_candidate": "Candidatos a compromiso", "agreement": "Acuerdos", "agreement_party": "Partes del acuerdo",
    "agreement_clause": "Cláusulas", "evidence": "Evidencias", "task": "Tareas",
}

ACTION_LABEL = {"APPROVE": "Aprobar", "EXPORT": "Exportar", "EDIT_ANY": "Editar lo ajeno", "VERIFY": "Verificar",
                "APPROVE_OVER_CAP": "Aprobar sobre el tope", "DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED": "Descargar restringidas"}

# the label rule of §3.8 gives a phrase that is too long, or not a noun phrase, for these: the shortened label
FIELD_LABEL = {
    ("project", "policy_workshop_commitments_blocked"): "Bloquear compromisos de talleres",
    ("project", "policy_senior_approval_for_dialogue"): "Aprobación superior en mesas de diálogo",
    ("project", "policy_internal_approval"): "Aprobación interna",
    ("project", "policy_no_jobs_in_easements"): "Sin empleo en servidumbres",
    ("project", "due_soon_days"): "Días para marcar por vencer",
    ("project", "policy_cap_blocks_approval"): "El tope bloquea la aprobación",
    ("project", "policy_synergy_requires_agreement"): "Sinergias con acuerdo",
    ("project", "policy_priority_warning"): "Advertir aportes fuera de prioridades",
    ("commitment", "code"): "Código del compromiso",
    ("commitment", "formality"): "Formalidad",
    ("commitment", "request_medium"): "Medio de solicitud",
    ("commitment", "amount"): "Monto",
    ("commitment", "currency"): "Moneda",
    ("commitment", "executed_amount"): "Monto ejecutado",
    ("commitment", "delay_risk_reported"): "Riesgo de retraso informado",
    ("commitment", "beneficiaries_detail"): "Detalle de los beneficiarios",
    ("commitment", "origin_justification"): "Justificación del origen",
    ("commitment", "requires_senior_approval"): "Requiere aprobación de nivel superior",
    ("commitment", "fulfilled_date"): "Fecha de cumplimiento",
    ("commitment", "closed_date"): "Fecha de cierre",
    ("commitment", "cancelled_date"): "Fecha de cancelación",
    ("commitment_reschedule", "agreed_with_stakeholder"): "Acordada con el grupo de interés",
    ("commitment_dependency", "blocking"): "Bloqueante",
    ("delivery", "delivered_items"): "Lo entregado",
    ("delivery", "conformity_given"): "Conformidad",
    ("delivery", "conformity_by"): "Quienes dan conformidad",
    ("commitment_candidate", "ai_generated"): "Propuesto por la IA",
    ("agreement", "code"): "Código del acuerdo",
    ("agreement_clause", "creates_commitment"): "Origina compromisos",
    ("evidence", "people_identifiable"): "Personas identificables",
    ("evidence", "photo_consent_given"): "Consentimiento de las personas fotografiadas",
    ("evidence", "import_batch_id"): "Lote de importación",
    ("task", "generator_key"): "Clave del generador",
}

RELATIONSHIP_LABEL = {
    "commitment_stakeholder": "Solicitantes",
    "candidate_created_commitment": "Compromiso creado",
    "candidate_duplicate_of": "Duplicado de",
    "evidence_project": "Proyecto",
    "evidence_replaces": "Reemplaza a",
}


def field_label(obj, field):
    if (obj, field["name"]) in FIELD_LABEL:
        return FIELD_LABEL[(obj, field["name"])]
    text = field["description"].replace("(derivado)", "").replace("(restringido)", "").replace('"', "").strip()
    text = d.strip_parens(text)
    text = re.split(r"; |: |\. |, |\.$", text)[0]
    return text.strip().rstrip(".")


def relationship_label(cards, plural, rel):
    if rel["name"] in RELATIONSHIP_LABEL:
        return RELATIONSHIP_LABEL[rel["name"]]
    # the links of an evidence name what it proves: the plural of the target
    if rel["name"].startswith("evidence_") and rel["type"] == "MANY_TO_MANY":
        return plural[rel["target"]]
    if rel["note"] == "—":
        return cards[rel["target"]].label
    return d.strip_parens(rel["note"].split(";")[0]).strip()


def new_field(name, f):
    out = {"name": f["name"], "label": field_label(name, f), "type": f["type"]}
    if f["type"] == "ENUM":
        out["enum"] = f["enum"]
    if f["type"] == "GEOMETRY":
        out.update({"geometryType": f["geometryType"], "srid": 4326, "dimension": 2})
    if f["name"] in CARDS[name].content_types:
        out["contentTypes"] = CARDS[name].content_types[f["name"]]
    if f["required"]:
        out["required"] = True
    if f["unique"]:
        out["unique"] = True
    if f["name"] in CARDS[name].indexed:
        out["indexed"] = True
    if f["derived"]:
        out["derived"] = True
    if f["default"] is not None:
        out["defaultValue"] = f["default"]
    out["description"] = f["description"].replace("`", "")
    return out


def build():
    existing = json.load(open(os.path.join("model", "model.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    old_objects = {o["name"]: o for o in existing["objects"]}
    old_rels = {r["name"]: r for r in existing["relationships"]}
    catalog = d.matrix()
    plural = {o["name"]: o["pluralLabel"] for o in existing["objects"]} | PLURAL
    objects, relationships, used = [], [], set()
    for name, card in CARDS.items():
        if card.phase > PHASE or name not in catalog:
            continue
        old = old_objects.get(name)
        old_fields = {f["name"]: f for f in old["fields"]} if old else {}
        fields = [old_fields.get(f["name"]) or new_field(name, f) for f in card.fields if d.field_phase(card, f) <= PHASE]
        used |= {f["enum"] for f in fields if f["type"] == "ENUM"}
        if old:
            obj = {k: v for k, v in old.items() if k != "fields"}
        else:
            obj = {"name": name, "label": card.label, "pluralLabel": PLURAL[name],
                   "description": card.description.replace("`", "")}
            obj.update({flag: True for flag, on in card.flags.items() if on})
            if card.unique_constraints:
                obj["uniqueConstraints"] = card.unique_constraints
            if card.indexes:
                obj["indexes"] = card.indexes
            if card.actions:
                obj["actions"] = [{"name": a, "label": ACTION_LABEL[a]} for a in card.actions]
        obj["fields"] = fields
        objects.append(obj)
        for rel in card.relationships:
            if rel["inverse"] or d.relationship_phase(CARDS, card, rel) > PHASE:
                continue
            if rel["name"] in old_rels:
                relationships.append(old_rels[rel["name"]])
                continue
            out = {"name": rel["name"]}
            if rel["type"] == "MANY_TO_MANY":
                out["type"] = "MANY_TO_MANY"
            out.update({"label": relationship_label(CARDS, plural, rel), "inverseLabel": plural[name], "source": name,
                        "target": rel["target"]})
            if rel["type"] == "MANY_TO_ONE":
                out["fieldName"] = rel["field"]
                if rel["required"]:
                    out["required"] = True
            relationships.append(out)
    catalogs = d.catalogs()
    enums = {key: existing["enums"].get(key, catalogs[key]) for key in sorted(used)}
    return {"enums": enums, "objects": objects, "relationships": relationships}


def dump(model):
    """The hand layout of model.json: one enum, field or relationship per line."""
    line = lambda value: json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)  # noqa: E731
    out = ["{", '  "enums": {']
    keys = list(model["enums"])
    for i, key in enumerate(keys):
        out.append(f"    {line(key)}: {line(model['enums'][key])}{',' if i < len(keys) - 1 else ''}")
    out.append("  },")
    out.append('  "objects": [')
    for i, obj in enumerate(model["objects"]):
        out.append("    {")
        for k in [k for k in obj if k != "fields"]:
            out.append(f"      {line(k)}: {line(obj[k])},")
        out.append('      "fields": [')
        for j, field in enumerate(obj["fields"]):
            out.append(f"        {line(field)}{',' if j < len(obj['fields']) - 1 else ''}")
        out.append("      ]")
        out.append("    }" + ("," if i < len(model["objects"]) - 1 else ""))
    out.append("  ],")
    out.append('  "relationships": [')
    for i, rel in enumerate(model["relationships"]):
        out.append(f"    {line(rel)}{',' if i < len(model['relationships']) - 1 else ''}")
    out.append("  ]")
    out.append("}")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


CARDS = d.cards()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.stdout.write(dump(build()))
```

```json
{
  "name": "wf-i-agreement",
  "label": "Acuerdo",
  "enabled": true,
  "definition": {
    "states": [
      { "name": "draft", "label": "Borrador", "type": "INITIAL" },

      { "name": "cancelled", "label": "Anulado", "type": "FINAL" }
    ],
    "transitions": [
      { "name": "start_negotiation", "label": "Iniciar negociación", "from": "draft", "to": "in_negotiation",
```

- [ ] **Paso 7: Guardar el generador de los roles**

Crear `$SCRATCH/gen_roles_f3.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""One-shot generator of model/roles.json for phase F3 (plan 3.1, Tarea 7).

Run from the repository root, after model/model.json has the F3 objects:
python3 <this file> > model/roles.f3.json && mv model/roles.f3.json model/roles.json
Keeps the names, labels and descriptions of roles.json (but the two roles that get their first grants in F3), gives each
role the grants of security.md §7.2 to §7.8 for the objects of model.json, from the later of the role's phase and the
object's (§7.1), and rebuilds restrictedFields from §7.9 for the objects of model.json. Stdlib only.
"""
import json
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "model"))
import design_docs as d  # noqa: E402

PHASE = "F3"

# the descriptions said "Recibe permisos desde F3."; from F3 on they say what the role does
DESCRIPTION = {
    "COMMITMENT_MANAGER": "Lleva la matriz de compromisos: los registra, entrega, verifica y cierra.",
    "APPROVER": "Aprueba compromisos con la acción declarada APPROVE, sin editarlos.",
}


def build():
    with open("model/roles.json", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        current = json.load(f)
    with open("model/model.json", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        model = json.load(f)
    phases, _ = d.role_phases()
    cards = d.cards()
    matrix = d.matrix()
    names = [o["name"] for o in model["objects"]]
    for role in current["roles"]:
        name = role["name"]
        if name in DESCRIPTION:
            role["description"] = DESCRIPTION[name]
        if name == "AUDITOR":
            continue
        grants = {}
        for obj in names:
            if max(phases[name], cards[obj].phase) > PHASE:
                continue
            cell = matrix[obj][name]
            if cell:
                grants[obj] = cell
        role["permisos"] = grants
    restricted = []
    for obj, field, readers in d.restricted_fields():
        if obj not in names:
            continue
        readers = [r for r in readers if r in phases]
        same = next((e for e in restricted if e["object"] == obj and e["readers"] == readers), None)
        if same:
            same["fields"].append(field)
        else:
            restricted.append({"object": obj, "fields": [field], "readers": readers})
    current["restrictedFields"] = restricted
    return current


def dump(roles):
    line = lambda value: json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)  # noqa: E731
    out = ["{", f'  "mappedToAdmin": {line(roles["mappedToAdmin"])},', '  "roles": [']
    for i, role in enumerate(roles["roles"]):
        out.append("    {")
        out.append(f'      "name": {line(role["name"])},')
        out.append(f'      "label": {line(role["label"])},')
        out.append(f'      "description": {line(role["description"])},')
        if role["permisos"]:
            out.append('      "permisos": {')
            items = list(role["permisos"].items())
            for j, (obj, grants) in enumerate(items):
                out.append(f"        {line(obj)}: {line(grants)}{',' if j < len(items) - 1 else ''}")
            out.append("      }")
        else:
            out.append('      "permisos": {}')
        out.append("    }" + ("," if i < len(roles["roles"]) - 1 else ""))
    out.append("  ],")
    out.append('  "restrictedFields": [')
    for i, entry in enumerate(roles["restrictedFields"]):
        out.append(f"    {line(entry)}{',' if i < len(roles['restrictedFields']) - 1 else ''}")
    out.append("  ]")
    out.append("}")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.stdout.write(dump(build()))
```

```kotlin
    protected fun requiresReason(objectName: String): Boolean = Model.objects.getValue(objectName)["requiresReason"] == true

    // una subida preparada para el campo FILE `field` (WAS-ADR-0061), como `account` (el admin si es null): su id, que
    // adjunta el archivo en el alta de quien lo subió dentro de la mitad de wasichai.files.cleanup.delay
    protected fun stagedFile(
        objectName: String,
        field: String,
        account: Account? = null
    ): String {
        val parts = MultipartBodyBuilder().apply { part("file", PDF).filename("prueba.pdf") }.build()
        val body =
            client
                .post()
                .uri("/api/objects/$objectName/files/$field")
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, account?.token ?: token)
                .contentType(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
                .body(BodyInserters.fromMultipartData(parts))
                .exchange()
                .expectStatus()
                .isCreated
                .expectBody(String::class.java)
                .returnResult()
                .responseBody!!
        return tree(body)["id"].asString()
    }
```

- [ ] **Paso 1: Escribir la base y las pruebas**

Crear `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/F3ApiTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe

import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import sgspe.common.Records
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode
import wasichai.core.data.RecordService
import java.util.UUID

// la base de las pruebas de integración de F3 (plan 3.1; el plan 3.2 la usa para las suyas): lo de DomainApiTest más los
// registros de compromisos, acuerdos y tareas que ya cumplen las guardas de 3.2 (D1 y D2 de WF-D, la de AgreementWriteGuard
// para llegar a `active`, un único origen de la tarea), las altas de los objetos apiOnly como la plataforma (delivery,
// commitment_candidate, commitment_reschedule: sus endpoints son del plan 3.2) y la espera de una automatización, que
// corre fuera de la petición (WAS-ADR-0016)
abstract class F3ApiTest : DomainApiTest() {
    @Autowired
    private lateinit var records: Records

    @Autowired
    private lateinit var recordService: RecordService

    protected fun organizationId(): UUID = UUID.fromString(me()["organizationId"].asString())

    // un registro escrito como la plataforma, con la marca SgspeWrite y su motivo, como lo harán los endpoints de 3.2: la
    // única vía de alta de un objeto apiOnly en estas pruebas (nunca un campo FILE: la plataforma no adjunta archivos)
    protected fun platformCreate(
        objectName: String,
        attributes: Map<String, Any?>,
        reason: String = "alta de prueba como la plataforma"
    ): String = runBlocking { recordService.asPlatform(organizationId()) { records.create(objectName, attributes, reason) } }.id.toString()

    // los campos que WF-D exige para salir de draft y para aprobar (D1, D2), con un verificador distinto del responsable (D11)
    protected fun commitmentAttributes(
        project: String,
        owner: Account,
        verifier: Account
    ): Map<String, Any?> =
        mapOf(
            "project" to project,
            "code" to uniqueName("COM-"),
            "title" to "Mejora del canal de riego",
            "description" to "Mejora del canal de riego de la comunidad.",
            "objective" to "Asegurar el riego de la campaña agrícola.",
            "resource_type" to "MATERIAL",
            "quantity" to 120,
            "unit" to "metros lineales",
            "request_medium" to "ACUERDO EN REUNION",
            "agreed_date" to "2026-10-01",
            "due_date" to "2026-12-15",
            "amount" to 15000,
            "currency" to "PEN",
            "beneficiaries_count" to 40,
            "owner_user_id" to owner.id,
            "owner_user_email" to owner.email,
            "verifier_user_id" to verifier.id,
            "verifier_user_email" to verifier.email
        )

    // un compromiso en draft listo para enviar y aprobar: lo crea `creator` (el admin si es null) y lleva un grupo de interés
    // solicitante (commitment_stakeholder, D1). el vínculo lo hace el admin con motivo: commitment es requiresReason
    protected fun commitment(
        project: String,
        owner: Account,
        verifier: Account,
        creator: Account? = null,
        attributes: Map<String, Any?> = emptyMap()
    ): String {
        val id =
            createRecord(
                "commitment",
                commitmentAttributes(project, owner, verifier) + attributes,
                mapOf(REASON to reason("alta de prueba")),
                creator?.token ?: token
            )
        val linked = linkWithReason("commitment", id, "commitment_stakeholder", stakeholder(project))
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT, linked.status, linked.body)
        return id
    }

    // un vínculo con la cabecera de motivo, que piden los extremos requiresReason (WAS-ADR-0041)
    protected fun linkWithReason(
        objectName: String,
        id: String,
        relationship: String,
        otherId: String,
        token: String = this.token
    ): Response =
        exchange(
            "POST",
            "/api/objects/$objectName/records/$id/related/$relationship",
            mapOf("otherId" to otherId),
            token,
            mapOf(REASON to reason("vínculo de prueba"))
        )

    // un acuerdo en draft que AgreementWriteGuard deja llegar a `active`: un CONVENIO firmado y vigente
    protected fun agreement(
        project: String,
        creator: Account? = null,
        attributes: Map<String, Any?> = emptyMap()
    ): String =
        createRecord(
            "agreement",
            mapOf(
                "project" to project,
                "code" to uniqueName("ACU-"),
                "title" to "Convenio de cooperación",
                "agreement_type" to "CONVENIO",
                "signed_date" to "2026-09-30",
                "effective_date" to "2026-10-01",
                "expiry_date" to "2027-09-30"
            ) + attributes,
            mapOf(REASON to reason("alta de prueba")),
            creator?.token ?: token
        )

    // una tarea con un único origen (ExactlyOneGuard): una interacción del proyecto, que leen todos los roles que crean tareas
    protected fun task(
        project: String,
        creator: Account? = null,
        attributes: Map<String, Any?> = emptyMap()
    ): String =
        createRecord(
            "task",
            mapOf("project" to project, "title" to "Coordinar la visita", "engagement" to engagement(project)) + attributes,
            token = creator?.token ?: token
        )

    // la ejecución de la automatización `name` para el registro `recordId`, cuando terminó (SUCCEEDED, FAILED o SKIPPED):
    // el drain de wasichai-automation la toma fuera de la petición, cada wasichai.automation.poll-interval (1 s)
    protected fun awaitRun(
        objectName: String,
        name: String,
        recordId: String,
        seconds: Long = 30
    ): JsonNode {
        val deadline = System.nanoTime() + seconds * 1_000_000_000
        while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
            val run =
                tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/$objectName/automations/$name/runs?limit=50", null, HttpStatus.OK))
                    .values()
                    .firstOrNull { it["recordId"]?.asString() == recordId && it["status"].asString() in FINISHED }
            if (run != null) return run
            Thread.sleep(250)
        }
        throw AssertionError("$name no terminó en $seconds s para $objectName $recordId")
    }

    companion object {
        private val FINISHED = setOf("SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "SKIPPED")
    }
}
```

```kotlin
    fun `los receptores de una entrega y la cita de un candidato solo los ven sus lectores`() {
        // delivery y commitment_candidate son apiOnly: los crea la plataforma, como harán los endpoints del plan 3.2
        val commitment = commitment(testProject(), owner = member("COMMUNITY_RELATIONS_OFFICER"), verifier = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER"))

                    "commitment" to commitment,
                    "delivery_date" to "2026-11-20",

                    "recipients" to "Persona Ejemplo 6 y Persona Ejemplo 7",
                    "conformity_by" to "Persona Ejemplo 6"
                )
            )
        val candidate =
            platformCreate(
                "commitment_candidate",
                mapOf(
                    "project" to testProject(),
                    "proposed_text" to "Arreglar el canal",
                    "ai_generated" to false,
                    "evidence_quote" to "[PERSONA_1] pidió el canal"
                )
            )
        val readOnly = userWithRole("READ_ONLY")
        val approver = userWithRole("APPROVER")
        val manager = userWithRole("COMMITMENT_MANAGER")
        val forReadOnly = record("delivery", delivery, readOnly)
        assertFalse(forReadOnly.has("recipients") || forReadOnly.has("conformity_by"), forReadOnly.toString())
        assertEquals("2026-11-20", forReadOnly["delivery_date"].asString())
        assertEquals("Persona Ejemplo 6", record("delivery", delivery, manager)["conformity_by"].asString())
        // la cita la leen quienes descargan evidencias restringidas (DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED); APPROVER no
        assertFalse(record("commitment_candidate", candidate, approver).has("evidence_quote"))
        assertEquals("[PERSONA_1] pidió el canal", record("commitment_candidate", candidate, manager)["evidence_quote"].asString())
    }
```

  (UPS-23 y §20.1), `docs/roadmap.md` (F3: «Workflows», «Entregables»; «Brechas upstream por fase»), `docs/domain-model.md`
  (documentos emitidos y automatizaciones)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `design_docs.automations()`, `document_types()` (Tarea 2); `apply.load_definitions` (Tarea 3); el modelo y los
  workflows (Tarea 4); `F3ApiTest.commitment`, `awaitRun` (Tarea 5).
- Produces: los tipos `meeting-minutes` y `attendance-list` de `engagement`; las automatizaciones
  `aut-01-commitment-execution-task`, `aut-05-meeting-minutes` y `aut-10-engagement-reschedule-task`; UPS-23.

```python
y nada de una fase posterior; cada workflow tiene los estados de domain-model.md §7.3 y las reglas del motor; cada
```

En `model/test_model.py`, reemplazar:

```python


if __name__ == "__main__":
```

por:

```python

def definitions(kind):
    """{object: [definition]} of model/<kind>/<object>.json, in file order."""
    folder = os.path.join(HERE, kind)
    if not os.path.isdir(folder):
        return {}
    return {f[:-len(".json")]: load(os.path.join(kind, f)) for f in sorted(os.listdir(folder)) if f.endswith(".json")}


class AutomationsAndDocumentTypesTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """workflows.md, «Automatizaciones» y «Pruebas del modelo»: las automatizaciones y los tipos de documento de la fase,
    sus disparadores, sus acciones y lo que imprimen (security.md §11.4)."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.model = load("model.json")
        cls.objects = {o["name"]: o for o in cls.model["objects"]}
        cls.cards = design_docs.cards()
        cls.automations = definitions("automations")
        cls.types = definitions("document-types")
        cls.workflows = {name: wf for name, wf in definitions("workflows").items()}
        cls.restricted = {(e["object"], f) for e in load("roles.json")["restrictedFields"] for f in e["fields"]}

    def test_las_automatizaciones_son_las_de_workflows_md_hasta_la_fase_salvo_las_que_esperan_una_brecha(self):
        doc = [a for a in design_docs.automations() if a["phase"] <= PHASE]
        wanted = sorted((a["object"], a["name"]) for a in doc if a["after"] is None)
        got = sorted((obj, a["name"]) for obj, items in self.automations.items() for a in items)
        self.assertEqual(got, wanted)
        # AUT-03 espera a UPS-23: el acta de entrega congelaría los receptores de delivery (workflows.md)
        self.assertEqual([(a["id"], a["after"]) for a in doc if a["after"]], [("AUT-03", "UPS-23")])

    def test_cada_disparador_es_el_de_workflows_md_y_nombra_un_estado_de_su_workflow(self):
        doc = {a["name"]: a for a in design_docs.automations()}
        for obj, items in self.automations.items():
            for automation in items:
                trigger = automation["definition"]["trigger"]
                wanted = doc[automation["name"]]
                self.assertEqual((trigger["type"], trigger.get("state"), trigger.get("transition")),
                                 (wanted["trigger"], wanted["state"], wanted["transition"]), automation["name"])
                if trigger.get("state"):
                    states = {st["name"] for st in self.workflows[obj]["definition"]["states"]}
                    self.assertIn(trigger["state"], states, automation["name"])

    def test_ninguna_escribe_un_derivado_y_cada_tarea_que_crea_lleva_proyecto_y_un_solo_origen(self):
        # workflows.md: ninguna automatización escribe un campo derivado; ExactlyOneGuard pide un único origen en task
        origins = self.cards["task"].exactly_one[0]
        for items in self.automations.values():
            for automation in items:
                for action in automation["definition"]["actions"]:
                    if action["type"] != "CREATE_RECORD":
                        continue
                    target = self.objects[action["targetObject"]]
                    derived = {f["name"] for f in target["fields"] if f.get("derived")}
                    self.assertEqual(set(action["values"]) & derived, set(), automation["name"])
                    if action["targetObject"] == "task":
                        self.assertEqual(action["values"]["project"], "{{project}}", automation["name"])
                        self.assertEqual(len([o for o in origins if o in action["values"]]), 1, automation["name"])

    def test_los_tipos_de_documento_son_los_de_workflows_md_hasta_la_fase_salvo_los_que_esperan_una_brecha(self):
        wanted = sorted((t["object"], t["name"], t["label"], t["prefix"]) for t in design_docs.document_types()
                        if t["phase"] <= PHASE and t["after"] is None)
        got = sorted((obj, t["name"], t["label"], t["prefix"]) for obj, items in self.types.items() for t in items)
        self.assertEqual(got, wanted)

    def test_cada_documento_generado_es_un_tipo_de_su_objeto(self):
        for obj, items in self.automations.items():
            names = {t["name"] for t in self.types.get(obj, [])}
            for automation in items:
                for action in automation["definition"]["actions"]:
                    if action["type"] == "GENERATE_DOCUMENT":
                        self.assertIn(action["documentType"], names, automation["name"])

    def has_restricted(self, obj):
        card = self.cards[obj]
        fields = {f["name"] for f in card.fields if f["restricted"]}
        fields |= {r["field"] for r in card.relationships if r["restricted"] and not r["inverse"]}
        return bool(fields) or any(o == obj for o, _ in self.restricted)

    def test_ningun_tipo_de_documento_congela_un_campo_restringido(self):
        # security.md §11.4 y UPS-23: la copia congelada lleva el registro entero y cada columna de las tablas
        # relacionadas que nombra la plantilla, sin filtrar por permisos de campo
        relationships = {r["name"]: r for r in self.model["relationships"]}

        def related(node):
            found = [node["attrs"]["relationship"]] if node["type"] == "relatedTable" else []
            for child in node.get("content", []):
                found.extend(related(child))
            return found

        for obj, items in self.types.items():
            self.assertFalse(self.has_restricted(obj), obj)
            for doc in items:
                for name in related(doc["template"]):
                    rel = relationships[name]
                    other = rel["source"] if rel["target"] == obj else rel["target"]
                    self.assertFalse(self.has_restricted(other), (doc["name"], name))

    def test_el_objeto_del_acta_de_entrega_tiene_campos_restringidos(self):
        # lo que espera UPS-23: si delivery dejara de restringir campos, el acta de entrega podría volver a F3
        self.assertTrue(self.has_restricted("delivery"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
```

```python
    def test_a_tenant_of_f2_moves_to_f3_adding_only_what_f3_brings(self):
        # Review Focus 1: el modelo de F2 sale de model.json con las fases de las fichas (168 creados y 11 conjuntos, como
        # el aprovisionamiento real de F2); subirlo a F3 agrega 12 objetos, 15 campos de project, 6 acciones y 46
        # relaciones, pone los únicos de 4 objetos nuevos y no toca nada de F2
        core = FakeCore()
        self.addCleanup(core.stop)
        login = ["--core", core.base_url, "--email", "admin-tenant@sgspe.test", "--password", "clave-del-tenant"]
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            f2 = quiet(apply.main, ["--model", write_model(folder, f2_subset(load(MODEL_PATH))), *login])
        self.assertEqual(f2[0], 0, f2)
        self.assertIn("done: 168 created, 11 updated, 0 skipped", f2[1])
        core.requests.clear()
        f3 = quiet(apply.main, ["--model", MODEL_PATH, *login])
        self.assertEqual(f3[0], 0, f3)
        self.assertIn("done: 79 created, 4 updated, 167 skipped", f3[1])
        touched = {p.split("/")[3] for m, p, _, _ in core.requests if m == "PUT" and p.startswith("/api/objects/")}
        self.assertEqual(touched, {"commitment", "commitment_dependency", "agreement", "agreement_clause"})
        added = [line for line in f3[1].splitlines() if line.startswith("add    field")]
        self.assertEqual(len(added), 15)
        self.assertTrue(all(line.startswith("add    field project.") for line in added), added)


def f2_subset(model):
    """model.json as it was in F2: the objects, fields, relationships and ENUM keys whose phase is F2 by the cards."""
    import design_docs
    cards = design_docs.cards()
    objects = []
    for obj in model["objects"]:
        card = cards[obj["name"]]
        if card.phase > "F2":
            continue
        phases = {f["name"]: design_docs.field_phase(card, f) for f in card.fields}
        objects.append({**obj, "fields": [f for f in obj["fields"] if phases[f["name"]] <= "F2"]})
    names = {o["name"] for o in objects}
    relationships = [r for r in model["relationships"] if r["source"] in names and r["target"] in names
                     and design_docs.relationship_phase(cards, cards[r["source"]],
                                                        next(x for x in cards[r["source"]].relationships
                                                             if x["name"] == r["name"])) <= "F2"]
    used = {f["enum"] for o in objects for f in o["fields"] if f["type"] == "ENUM"}
    return {"enums": {k: v for k, v in model["enums"].items() if k in used}, "objects": objects,
            "relationships": relationships}
```

Crear `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/workflow/AutomationsApiTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.workflow

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import sgspe.F3ApiTest

// workflows.md, «Pruebas de trabajos, listeners y automatizaciones»: cada automatización de F3 deja una ejecución
// SUCCEEDED con el registro o el documento esperado y su motivo `automation '<nombre>'`, y ninguna escribe un derivado.
// AUT-01 crea la tarea de ejecución de un compromiso aprobado, AUT-10 la de una interacción cancelada y AUT-05 emite la
// minuta y la lista de asistentes de un acta aprobada, sin congelar los datos de los asistentes (security.md §11.4).
// AUT-03, el acta de entrega, espera a UPS-23
class AutomationsApiTest : F3ApiTest() {
    // la tarea que creó una automatización para el registro `origin` del campo `field`
    private fun createdTask(
        field: String,
        origin: String
    ): Map<String, Any?> {
        val tasks = tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/task/records?$field=$origin", null, HttpStatus.OK))["content"].values()
        assertEquals(1, tasks.size, "tareas de $field $origin: $tasks")
        return attributesOf(tasks.single()) + ("id" to tasks.single()["id"].asString())
    }

    private fun reasonOf(
        objectName: String,
        id: String
    ): String? =
        tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/$objectName/records/$id/history", null, HttpStatus.OK))
            .values()
            .first()["reason"]
            ?.asString()

    @Test
    fun `AUT-01 crea la tarea de ejecucion del compromiso aprobado para su responsable`() {
        val manager = member("COMMITMENT_MANAGER")
        val owner = member("COMMUNITY_RELATIONS_OFFICER")
        val id = commitment(testProject(), owner = owner, verifier = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER"), creator = manager)
        move("commitment", id, "submit", manager.token, "matriz completa")
        move("commitment", id, "approve", member("APPROVER").token, "aprobado")

        val run = awaitRun("commitment", "aut-01-commitment-execution-task", id)
        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", run["status"].asString(), run.toString())
        val code = attributesOf(tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/commitment/records/$id", null, HttpStatus.OK)))["code"]
        val task = createdTask("commitment", id)
        assertEquals("Ejecutar compromiso $code", task["title"])
        assertEquals(testProject(), task["project"])
        assertEquals(owner.id to owner.email, task["assignee_user_id"] to task["assignee_user_email"])
        assertEquals("2026-12-15", task["due_date"])
        assertEquals(null, task["deadline_status"])
        assertEquals("open", state("task", task["id"] as String))
        assertEquals("automation 'aut-01-commitment-execution-task'", reasonOf("task", task["id"] as String))
    }

    @Test
    fun `AUT-10 crea la tarea de reprogramar una interaccion cancelada`() {
        val officer = member("COMMUNITY_RELATIONS_OFFICER")
        val id = engagement(testProject(), token = officer.token, attributes = mapOf("title" to "Taller de riego", "cancel_reason" to "Lluvias intensas"))
        move("engagement", id, "cancel_planned", officer.token, "lluvias intensas")

        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", awaitRun("engagement", "aut-10-engagement-reschedule-task", id)["status"].asString())
        val task = createdTask("engagement", id)
        assertEquals("Reprogramar o reemplazar la interacción Taller de riego", task["title"])
        assertEquals(testProject(), task["project"])
        assertEquals("automation 'aut-10-engagement-reschedule-task'", reasonOf("task", task["id"] as String))
    }

    @Test
    fun `AUT-05 emite la minuta y la lista de asistentes del acta aprobada sin los datos de los asistentes`() {
        val officer = member("COMMUNITY_RELATIONS_OFFICER")
        val social = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER")
        val id =
            engagement(
                testProject(),
                token = officer.token,
                attributes =
                    mapOf(
                        "title" to "Asamblea de la junta",
                        "scheduled_at" to "2026-10-08T15:00:00Z",
                        "held_at" to "2026-10-08T16:00:00Z",
                        "place" to "Local comunal",
                        "minutes" to "Se acordó mejorar el canal de riego."
                    )
            )
        participant(id, officer.token, mapOf("participant_name" to "Persona Ejemplo 9", "phone" to "900000009", "represented_people_count" to 2))
        for (step in listOf("schedule", "register_held", "draft_minutes", "submit_minutes")) move("engagement", id, step, officer.token)
        move("engagement", id, "approve_minutes", social.token)

        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", awaitRun("engagement", "aut-05-meeting-minutes", id)["status"].asString())
        val documents = tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/engagement/records/$id/documents", null, HttpStatus.OK)).values()
        assertEquals(setOf("MR", "LA"), documents.map { it["number"].asString().substringBefore("-") }.toSet())
        for (document in documents) {
            assertEquals("VALID", document["status"].asString())
            val snapshot = document["snapshot"]
            assertEquals("Asamblea de la junta", snapshot["values"]["title"].asString())
            // security.md §11.4: nada de los asistentes llega a la copia congelada, ni una tabla relacionada
            assertTrue(snapshot["related"].isEmpty, snapshot["related"].toString())
            assertFalse(document.toString().contains("Persona Ejemplo 9") || document.toString().contains("900000009"), document.toString())
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun `las automatizaciones aplicadas son las de model y ninguna se queda sin correr`() {
        val names =
            listOf("commitment", "engagement").flatMap { objectName ->
                tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/$objectName/automations", null, HttpStatus.OK)).values().map { it["name"].asString() }
            }
        assertEquals(
            setOf("aut-01-commitment-execution-task", "aut-05-meeting-minutes", "aut-10-engagement-reschedule-task"),
            names.toSet()
        )
        val types = tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/engagement/document-types", null, HttpStatus.OK)).values()
        assertEquals(mapOf("meeting-minutes" to "MR", "attendance-list" to "LA"), types.associate { it["name"].asString() to it["prefix"].asString() })
        assertTrue(tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/delivery/document-types", null, HttpStatus.OK)).isEmpty, "el acta de entrega espera a UPS-23")
    }
}
```

En `docs/security.md`, reemplazar:

```markdown
- **Documentos emitidos** de wasichai-documents (RQ-REP-029): no se filtran por permisos de campo, porque quien ve el
  documento lo ve entero [WAS-DOC api/rest.md] [WAS-ADR-0023]. Regla: una plantilla imprime un campo restringido solo si
  todo rol con READ sobre el objeto puede leerlo. El acta de entrega deja espacios para los nombres y las firmas en lugar
  de imprimir `recipients`, y la ficha de grupo de interés no imprime la postura.
```

por:

```markdown
- **Documentos emitidos** de wasichai-documents (RQ-REP-029): no se filtran por permisos de campo, porque quien ve el
  documento lo ve entero [WAS-DOC api/rest.md] [WAS-ADR-0023]. Además, la copia congelada lleva **todos** los campos del
  registro y todas las columnas de cada tabla relacionada que nombra la plantilla, aunque no los imprima, y
  `GET /api/documents/{id}` la devuelve a todo rol con READ sobre el objeto (código de wasichai-documents 0.6.0, UPS-23).
  Regla: un tipo de documento solo se declara sobre un objeto sin campos restringidos y sin tablas relacionadas de
  objetos con campos restringidos; `test_model.py` lo comprueba. Por eso, hasta UPS-23, el acta de entrega (`delivery`
  restringe `recipients` y `conformity_by`) no es un documento emitido, y la lista de asistentes imprime el total y deja
  filas en blanco para los nombres y las firmas en lugar de la tabla de `engagement_participant`.
```

```markdown
| UPS-22 | Que las escrituras de la API genérica (`RecordService.create`, `update` y `delete`) corran en una transacción de la petición, a la que se unan las guardas y los listeners ([WAS-ADR-0038]), o que ofrezcan un gancho para que una guarda retenga un candado hasta el commit | Una guarda que lee el estado de otro registro con un candado lo suelta al volver, y la escritura ocurre después: un miembro entra en una versión de área de influencia recién aprobada, o sale de una aprobada y la deja sin miembros (RN-TER-003); lo mismo ocurre con un cambio de la identidad de un borrador (proyecto, tipo o número de versión) que se confirma justo después de la aprobación | `TerritoryWriteGuard` y la aprobación toman el mismo candado `area_of_influence:<id>`, lo que estrecha la ventana sin cerrarla (riesgo 16 de `architecture.md` §14); el mismo caso se repetirá en toda guarda que lea otro registro | F2 | RN-TER-003, RNF-INT-003 |
```

por:

```markdown
| UPS-23 | Que un documento emitido de wasichai-documents congele solo lo que su plantilla nombra, o respete los permisos de campo de quien lo lee: hoy su copia guarda todos los campos del registro y todas las columnas de las tablas relacionadas, y `GET /api/documents/{id}` la devuelve a todo rol con READ sobre el objeto | Un documento de un objeto con campos restringidos los entrega a quien no los lee: el acta de entrega de `delivery` (`recipients`, `conformity_by`) o una lista de asistentes con la tabla de `engagement_participant` (RQ-SEC-010) | Los tipos de documento solo se declaran sobre objetos sin campos restringidos (`security.md` §11.4): el acta de entrega (AUT-03) espera, y el acta firmada se sube como evidencia ACTA DE ENTREGA; la lista de asistentes deja filas en blanco para los nombres y las firmas | F3 | RQ-REP-029, RQ-SEC-010 |
| UPS-22 | Que las escrituras de la API genérica (`RecordService.create`, `update` y `delete`) corran en una transacción de la petición, a la que se unan las guardas y los listeners ([WAS-ADR-0038]), o que ofrezcan un gancho para que una guarda retenga un candado hasta el commit | Una guarda que lee el estado de otro registro con un candado lo suelta al volver, y la escritura ocurre después: un miembro entra en una versión de área de influencia recién aprobada, o sale de una aprobada y la deja sin miembros (RN-TER-003); lo mismo ocurre con un cambio de la identidad de un borrador (proyecto, tipo o número de versión) que se confirma justo después de la aprobación | `TerritoryWriteGuard` y la aprobación toman el mismo candado `area_of_influence:<id>`, lo que estrecha la ventana sin cerrarla (riesgo 16 de `architecture.md` §14); el mismo caso se repetirá en toda guarda que lea otro registro | F2 | RN-TER-003, RNF-INT-003 |
```
